$(document).ready(function(){
   ambil_tanggal("tanggal_lahir");
   
   $("#frm").submit(function(){
        if(frm.nip.value == "" || frm.nama_pegawai.value == "" || frm.id_jenis_kepegawaian.value == 0 || frm.id_kedudukan_kepegawaian.value == 0 || frm.id_status_kepegawaian.value == 0 || frm.id_agama.value == 0 || frm.id_jenis_kelamin.value == 0){
			var pesan ="Maaf!! Data dibawah ini harus diisi : <ol>";
			if(frm.nip.value == "" ){
				pesan += "<li>NIP</li>";
				document.getElementById("nip").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			} 
			if( frm.nama_pegawai.value == "" ){
				pesan += "<li>Nama Pegawai</li>";
				document.getElementById("nama_pegawai").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			} 
			if( frm.id_jenis_kepegawaian.value == 0 ){
				pesan += "<li>Jenis Kepegawaian</li>" ;
				document.getElementById("id_jenis_kepegawaian").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			} 
			if( frm.id_kedudukan_kepegawaian.value == 0 ){
				pesan += "<li>Kedudukan Kepegawaian</li>" ;
				document.getElementById("id_kedudukan_kepegawaian").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			} 
			if( frm.id_status_kepegawaian.value == 0 ){
				pesan += "<li>Status Kepegawaian</li>" ;
				document.getElementById("id_status_kepegawaian").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			} 
			if( frm.id_agama.value == 0 ){
				pesan +=  "<li>Agama</li>";
				document.getElementById("id_agama").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			} 
			if( frm.id_jenis_kelamin.value == 0){
				pesan += "<li>Jenis Kelamin</li>" ;
				document.getElementById("id_jenis_kelamin").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			}
			pesan += "<ol>";
            jAlert(pesan, "PERHATIAN");
            return false;
        }
        
   }); 
   
   	$("#input_password").dialog({

function upload_pasfoto(){ 
		$("#id_pegawai_5").val(id_pegawai);
		$("#pnl_upload_1").dialog("open");
}

function upload_karpeg(){
		$("#id_pegawai_6").val(id_pegawai); 
		$("#pnl_upload_2").dialog("open");
}

function upload_konversi_nip(){
		$("#id_pegawai_8").val(id_pegawai); 
		$("#pnl_upload_4").dialog("open");
}

function upload_ktp(){
		$("#id_pegawai_7").val(id_pegawai); 
		$("#pnl_upload_3").dialog("open");
}

		$("#id_pegawai_9").val(id_pegawai); 
		$("#pnl_upload_5").dialog("open");
}

		$("#id_pegawai_13").val(id_pegawai);

}

function catatan_upload(id_pegawai){
	$("#id_pegawai2").val(id_pegawai);
	$("#catatan_karpeg_dan_pasfoto").html(catatan_karpeg_dan_pasfoto);
	$("#pnl_catatan").dialog("open");
}

function something_wrong(what_is_wrong) {
        jAlert(what_is_wrong, "PERHATIAN");
}

function success(ada){
    jAlert("Data pegawai berhasil disimpan", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai";
	}); 
}

function success_upload(){
    jAlert("File berhasil diupload", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai";
	}); 
}

function show_password_oto(){
	$("#id_pegawaix").val(id_pegawai);
	$("#input_password").dialog("open");
}

function hold(){
    jAlert("Data sementara berhasil disimpan untuk diotorisasi", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai";
	}); 
}   

function approval(id_app){
	$("#id_approval").val(id_app);
	if(id_app==1){
	jAlert("<b>Data akan diotorisasi dahulu oleh pejabat berwenang</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			$("#frm_edit").submit();
		});
	}else if(id_app==0){
		$("#frm_edit").submit();
	}
}

 function success_catatan(){
	jAlert("<b>Catatan berhasil disimpan.</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai&id_ver=99";
		});	
} 

function lanjut(){
		upload_pasfoto();
}

/*Fungsi Untuk Hapus*/
 function notif(){
    jAlert("<b>Data Hapus Ini Akan diVerifikasi Terlebih Dahulu Oleh Admin</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai";
    });
}
 function success_hapus(){
    jAlert("<b>Data Berhasil Dihapus</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai";
    });
}

 function hapus_pasfoto(){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin akan menghapus file upload ini?", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(r){
			var modul_user = 16;
			if(modul_user==1 || modul_user==100)
				jAlert("<b>Data Hapus Ini Akan diVerifikasi Terlebih Dahulu Oleh Admin</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
					document.location.href = "php/upload_data/hapus_pasfoto2.php?id_pegawai=" + id_pegawai;
				});
			else
				jAlert("<b>Data Berhasil Dihapus</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
					document.location.href = "php/upload_data/hapus_pasfoto2.php?id_pegawai=" + id_pegawai;
				});
		}
    });
 }

			<script type="text/javascript">
$(document).ready(function(){
    $( "#pnl_upload" ).dialog({
            autoOpen: false,
    		height: "auto",
    		width: "800",
    		modal: true,
            show: "fade",
    		hide: "fade"
    });
	$( "#pnl_catatan" ).dialog({
            autoOpen: false,
    		height: "auto",
    		width: "800",
    		modal: true,
            show: "fade",
    		hide: "fade"
    });
	$("#input_password").dialog({
		autoOpen: false,
		height: "auto",
		width: "800",
		modal: true,
		show: "fade",
		hide: "fade"
	});	
});

function upload(id_pendidikan){
	$("#id_pendidikan").val(id_pendidikan);
    $("#pnl_upload").dialog("open");
}

function catatan_upload(id_pendidikan,catatan_pend){
	$("#id_pendidikan_catatan").val(id_pendidikan);
	$("#catatan_upload").html(catatan_pend);
    $("#pnl_catatan").dialog("open");
} 

function show_password_oto(id_riwayat){
	var id_pegawai = "25509";
	$("#id_pegawaix").val(id_pegawai);
	$("#id_riwayatx").val(id_riwayat);
	$("#input_password").dialog("open");
}

function hold(){
    jAlert("Data sementara berhasil disimpan untuk diotorisasi", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan";
	}); 
}

function success(id){ //id dari otorisasi=3
    jAlert("Data berhasil dihapus", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan";
	}); 
}

function approval(id_app){
	$("#id_approval").val(id_app);
	if(id_app==1){
	jAlert("<b>Data akan diotorisasi dahulu oleh pejabat berwenang</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			$("#frm_edit").submit();
		});
	}else if(id_app==0){
		$("#frm_edit").submit();
	}
}

function success_upload(){
    jAlert("<b>File scan SK telah diupload</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan";
    });
} 

function update_upload(){
    jAlert("<b>File Anda Telah Diupdate</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan";
    });
}

function success_catatan(){
	var id_ver = 0;
	 jAlert("<b>Catatan tolak anda berhasil disimpan</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(id_ver==99)
			document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan&id_ver=99";
		else
			document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan";

		})
}

function edit(id_pendidikan){
	var id_ver="";
	if(id_ver==99){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan_edit&id_pendidikan=" + id_pendidikan + "&id_ver="+id_ver;
	}else{ 
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan_edit&id_pendidikan=" + id_pendidikan; 
	}
}
 
function hapus(id_pendidikan,status_spv){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin ingin menghapus data riwayat pendidikan ini?", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(r){
			var id_ver = 0;
			var modul_group = 4;
			var otorisasi = 0;
			if(id_ver==99 || modul_group==2 || status_spv<3 || (modul_group==1 && otorisasi==0)){
				jAlert("Data Berhasil Dihapus", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
				document.location.href="php/riwayat_pendidikan/riwayat_pendidikan_hapus.php?id_pendidikan=" + id_pendidikan + "&id_ver=" + id_ver;}
				);
			}else if(modul_group==1 && otorisasi==1){
				show_password_oto(id_pendidikan);
			}else{		
				jAlert("Data Akan dilakukan Verifikasi Terlebih Dahulu Oleh Admin", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
				document.location.href="php/riwayat_pendidikan/riwayat_pendidikan_hapus.php?id_pendidikan=" + id_pendidikan;
				});	
			}				
		}
	}); 
} 
 
function hapus_riwayat_tolak(){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin ingin menghapus seluruh riwayat tolak pendidikan dari BKD? ", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			document.location.href="php/riwayat_pendidikan/hapus_riwayat_tolak.php";
	});
}

function tambah_pendidikan(){
   document.location.href='?mod=riwayat_pendidikan_tambah';
}

function something_wrong(what_is_wrong){
    jAlert(what_is_wrong, "PERHATIAN");
}

var id_pegawai = "25509";

$(document).ready(function(){
    $( "#dialog_cadis" ).dialog({
        autoOpen: false,
		height: 540,
		width: 500,
		modal: true,
        show: "fade",
		hide: "fade"
    });
	$("#input_password").dialog({
            autoOpen: false,
    		height: "auto",
    		width: "800",
    		modal: true,
            show: "fade",
    		hide: "fade"
    });	
	$( "#pnl_upload" ).dialog({
		autoOpen: false,
		height: "auto",
		width: "800",
		modal: true,
		show: "fade",
		hide: "fade"
	});
	
	$( "#pnl_catatan" ).dialog({
		autoOpen: false,
		height: "auto",
		width: "800",
		modal: true,
		show: "fade",
		hide: "fade"
	});
});

function hold(){
    jAlert("Data sementara berhasil disimpan untuk diotorisasi", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pangkat";
	}); 
}   

function show_password_oto(id){
	$("#id_riwayatx").val(id);
	$("#id_pegawaix").val(id_pegawai);
	$("#input_password").dialog("open");
}   

function approval(id_app){
	$("#id_approval").val(id_app);
	if(id_app==1){
	jAlert("<b>Data akan diotorisasi dahulu oleh pejabat berwenang</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			$("#frm_edit").submit();
		});
	}else if(id_app==0){
		$("#frm_edit").submit();
	}
}


function upload(id_riwayat_pangkat){
	$("#id_riwayat_pangkat").val(id_riwayat_pangkat);
    $("#pnl_upload").dialog("open");
}

function catatan_upload(id_riwayat_pangkat_catatan,catatan_upload_pangkat){
		$("#id_riwayat_pangkat_catatan").val(id_riwayat_pangkat_catatan);
		$("#catatan_upload").html(catatan_upload_pangkat);
		$("#pnl_catatan").dialog("open");
}

function success(id){
	text='disimpan';
	if(id==3){
		text='dihapus';
	}
    jAlert("Data riwayat pangkat berhasil "+text, "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pangkat";
    });
}

function success_upload(){
    jAlert("<b>File scan SK telah diupload</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pangkat";
    });
}

function update_upload(){
    jAlert("<b>File Anda Telah Diupdate</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pangkat";
    });
}

function success_catatan(){
	var id_ver = 0;
	jAlert("Catatan Disimpan!", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(id_ver==99)
			document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pangkat&id_ver=99";
		else
			document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pangkat";
    });
}

function edit(id_riwayat_pangkat, status_supervisi){
	var id_ver="";
	if(id_ver==99){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pangkat_edit&id_riwayat_pangkat=" + id_riwayat_pangkat + "&id_ver="+id_ver;
		
	}else{ 
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pangkat_edit&id_riwayat_pangkat=" + id_riwayat_pangkat; 
	}
}

function hapus(id_riwayat_pangkat, status_supervisi){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin ingin menghapus data riwayat pangkat ini?", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(r){
			var id_ver = 0;
			var modul_group = 4;
			var otorisasi = 0;
			if(id_ver==99 || modul_group==2 || status_supervisi<3 || (modul_group==1 && otorisasi==0)){
				jAlert("Data Berhasil Dihapus", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
					document.location.href="php/riwayat_pangkat/riwayat_pangkat_hapus.php?id_riwayat_pangkat=" + id_riwayat_pangkat;
				});	
			}else if(modul_group==1 && otorisasi==1){
				show_password_oto(id_riwayat_pangkat);
			}else{	
				jAlert("Data Akan dilakukan Verifikasi Terlebih Dahulu Oleh Admin", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
					document.location.href="php/riwayat_pangkat/riwayat_pangkat_hapus.php?id_riwayat_pangkat=" + id_riwayat_pangkat;
				});	
			}
		}
	});
}

function hapus_riwayat_tolak(){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin ingin menghapus seluruh riwayat tolak pangkat dari BKD? ", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			document.location.href="php/riwayat_pangkat/hapus_riwayat_tolak.php";
	});
}

function catatan(id_riwayat_pangkat){
    $("#dialog_cadis").dialog("open");
    $.ajax({
        type: "GET",
        url: "ajax/cadis_spv_riwayat_pangkat.php",
        data: "id_riwayat_pangkat=" + id_riwayat_pangkat,
        success: function(r){
            $("#dialog_cadis").html(r);
        }
    });
}

function tambah_pangkat(){
    document.location.href='?mod=riwayat_pangkat_tambah';
}

function something_wrong(what_is_wrong){
    jAlert(what_is_wrong, "PERHATIAN");
}
</script>

			}else{
				jAlert("Data Akan dilakukan Verifikasi Terlebih Dahulu Oleh Admin", "PERHATIAN", function(r){

				});

		}

}

function tambah_jabatan(){
    document.location.href='?mod=riwayat_jabatan_tambah'; 
}

function something_wrong(what_is_wrong){
    jAlert(what_is_wrong, "PERHATIAN");
}

function integrasi(id_riwayat_jabatan){
	$("#id_riw_x").val(id_riwayat_jabatan);
	$("#integrasi").dialog("open");
}

function update_siasn(id_action){
	$("#id_action").val(id_action);
	if(id_action==1 || id_action==0){
		$("#frm_int").submit();
	}
}

function something_wrong(what_is_wrong) {
        jAlert(what_is_wrong, "PERHATIAN");
}

function success(ada){
    jAlert("Data pegawai berhasil disimpan", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai";
	}); 
}

function success_upload(){
    jAlert("File berhasil diupload", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai";
	}); 
}

function show_password_oto(){
	$("#id_pegawaix").val(id_pegawai);
	$("#input_password").dialog("open");
}

function hold(){
    jAlert("Data sementara berhasil disimpan untuk diotorisasi", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai";
	}); 
}   

function approval(id_app){
	$("#id_approval").val(id_app);
	if(id_app==1){
	jAlert("<b>Data akan diotorisasi dahulu oleh pejabat berwenang</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			$("#frm_edit").submit();
		});
	}else if(id_app==0){
		$("#frm_edit").submit();
	}
}

 function success_catatan(){
	jAlert("<b>Catatan berhasil disimpan.</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai&id_ver=99";
		});	
}

		upload_pasfoto();
}

    });
}

 function hapus_pasfoto(){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin akan menghapus file upload ini?", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(r){
			var modul_user = 16;
			if(modul_user==1 || modul_user==100)
				jAlert("<b>Data Hapus Ini Akan diVerifikasi Terlebih Dahulu Oleh Admin</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
					document.location.href = "php/upload_data/hapus_pasfoto2.php?id_pegawai=" + id_pegawai;
				});
			else
				jAlert("<b>Data Berhasil Dihapus</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
					document.location.href = "php/upload_data/hapus_pasfoto2.php?id_pegawai=" + id_pegawai;
				});
		}
    });
 }

function upload(id_pendidikan){
	$("#id_pendidikan").val(id_pendidikan);
    $("#pnl_upload").dialog("open");
}

function catatan_upload(id_pendidikan,catatan_pend){
	$("#id_pendidikan_catatan").val(id_pendidikan);
	$("#catatan_upload").html(catatan_pend);
    $("#pnl_catatan").dialog("open");
} 

function show_password_oto(id_riwayat){
	var id_pegawai = "19910";
	$("#id_pegawaix").val(id_pegawai);
	$("#id_riwayatx").val(id_riwayat);
	$("#input_password").dialog("open");
}

function hold(){
    jAlert("Data sementara berhasil disimpan untuk diotorisasi", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan";
	}); 
}

function success(id){ //id dari otorisasi=3
    jAlert("Data berhasil dihapus", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan";
	}); 
}

function approval(id_app){
	$("#id_approval").val(id_app);
	if(id_app==1){
	jAlert("<b>Data akan diotorisasi dahulu oleh pejabat berwenang</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			$("#frm_edit").submit();
		});
	}else if(id_app==0){
		$("#frm_edit").submit();
	}
}

function success_upload(){
    jAlert("<b>File scan SK telah diupload</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan";
    });
} 

function update_upload(){
    jAlert("<b>File Anda Telah Diupdate</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan";
    });
}

function success_catatan(){
	var id_ver = 0;
	 jAlert("<b>Catatan tolak anda berhasil disimpan</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(id_ver==99)
			document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan&id_ver=99";
		else
			document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan";

		})
}

function edit(id_pendidikan){
	var id_ver="";
	if(id_ver==99){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan_edit&id_pendidikan=" + id_pendidikan + "&id_ver="+id_ver;
	}else{ 
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan_edit&id_pendidikan=" + id_pendidikan; 
	}
}
 
function hapus(id_pendidikan,status_spv){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin ingin menghapus data riwayat pendidikan ini?", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(r){
			var id_ver = 0;
			var modul_group = 4;
			var otorisasi = 0;
			if(id_ver==99 || modul_group==2 || status_spv<3 || (modul_group==1 && otorisasi==0)){
				jAlert("Data Berhasil Dihapus", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
				document.location.href="php/riwayat_pendidikan/riwayat_pendidikan_hapus.php?id_pendidikan=" + id_pendidikan + "&id_ver=" + id_ver;}
				);
			}else if(modul_group==1 && otorisasi==1){
				show_password_oto(id_pendidikan);
			}else{		
				jAlert("Data Akan dilakukan Verifikasi Terlebih Dahulu Oleh Admin", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
				document.location.href="php/riwayat_pendidikan/riwayat_pendidikan_hapus.php?id_pendidikan=" + id_pendidikan;
				});	
			}				
		}
	}); 
} 
 
function hapus_riwayat_tolak(){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin ingin menghapus seluruh riwayat tolak pendidikan dari BKD? ", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			document.location.href="php/riwayat_pendidikan/hapus_riwayat_tolak.php";
	});
}

function tambah_pendidikan(){
   document.location.href='?mod=riwayat_pendidikan_tambah';
}

function something_wrong(what_is_wrong){
    jAlert(what_is_wrong, "PERHATIAN");
}

	}
}


function upload(id_riwayat_pangkat){
	$("#id_riwayat_pangkat").val(id_riwayat_pangkat);
    $("#pnl_upload").dialog("open");
}

function catatan_upload(id_riwayat_pangkat_catatan,catatan_upload_pangkat){
		$("#id_riwayat_pangkat_catatan").val(id_riwayat_pangkat_catatan);
		$("#catatan_upload").html(catatan_upload_pangkat);
		$("#pnl_catatan").dialog("open");
}

function success(id){
	text='disimpan';
	if(id==3){
		text='dihapus';
	}
    jAlert("Data riwayat pangkat berhasil "+text, "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pangkat";
    });
}

function success_upload(){
    jAlert("<b>File scan SK telah diupload</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pangkat";
    });
}

function update_upload(){
    jAlert("<b>File Anda Telah Diupdate</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pangkat";
    });
}

function success_catatan(){
	var id_ver = 0;
	jAlert("Catatan Disimpan!", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(id_ver==99)
			document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pangkat&id_ver=99";
		else
			document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pangkat";
    });
}

function edit(id_riwayat_pangkat, status_supervisi){
	var id_ver="";
	if(id_ver==99){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pangkat_edit&id_riwayat_pangkat=" + id_riwayat_pangkat + "&id_ver="+id_ver;
		
	}else{ 
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pangkat_edit&id_riwayat_pangkat=" + id_riwayat_pangkat; 
	}
}

function hapus(id_riwayat_pangkat, status_supervisi){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin ingin menghapus data riwayat pangkat ini?", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(r){
			var id_ver = 0;
			var modul_group = 4;
			var otorisasi = 0;
			if(id_ver==99 || modul_group==2 || status_supervisi<3 || (modul_group==1 && otorisasi==0)){
				jAlert("Data Berhasil Dihapus", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
					document.location.href="php/riwayat_pangkat/riwayat_pangkat_hapus.php?id_riwayat_pangkat=" + id_riwayat_pangkat;
				});	
			}else if(modul_group==1 && otorisasi==1){
				show_password_oto(id_riwayat_pangkat);
			}else{	
				jAlert("Data Akan dilakukan Verifikasi Terlebih Dahulu Oleh Admin", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
					document.location.href="php/riwayat_pangkat/riwayat_pangkat_hapus.php?id_riwayat_pangkat=" + id_riwayat_pangkat;
				});	
			}
		}
	});
}

function hapus_riwayat_tolak(){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin ingin menghapus seluruh riwayat tolak pangkat dari BKD? ", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			document.location.href="php/riwayat_pangkat/hapus_riwayat_tolak.php";
	});
}

function catatan(id_riwayat_pangkat){
    $("#dialog_cadis").dialog("open");
    $.ajax({
        type: "GET",
        url: "ajax/cadis_spv_riwayat_pangkat.php",
        data: "id_riwayat_pangkat=" + id_riwayat_pangkat,
        success: function(r){
            $("#dialog_cadis").html(r);
        }
    });
}

function tambah_pangkat(){
    document.location.href='?mod=riwayat_pangkat_tambah';
}

function something_wrong(what_is_wrong){
    jAlert(what_is_wrong, "PERHATIAN");
}

	if(id==3){
		text = "Data berhasil dihapus.";
	}else if(id==4){
		text = "Data berhasil diupdate ke SIASN.";
	}
    jAlert(text, "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan";
	}); 
}

	$("#id_riwayatx").val(id);
	$("#id_pegawaix").val(id_pegawai);
	$("#input_password").dialog("open");
}   

function approval(id_app){
	$("#id_approval").val(id_app);
	if(id_app==1){
	jAlert("<b>Data akan diotorisasi dahulu oleh pejabat berwenang</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			$("#frm_edit").submit();
		});
	}else if(id_app==0){
		$("#frm_edit").submit();
	}
}

function success_catatan(){
	var id_ver = 0;
    jAlert("Catatan Disimpan!", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(id_ver==99)
			document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan&id_ver=99";
		else
			document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan";
    });
}

	jAlert(info, "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan";
    });

function edit(id_riwayat_jabatan, status_supervisi){
    var id_ver=0;
	if(id_ver==99){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan_edit&id_riwayat_jabatan=" + id_riwayat_jabatan + "&id_ver="+id_ver;
	}else{
        document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan_edit&id_riwayat_jabatan=" + id_riwayat_jabatan;
	}
}

function duplikasi(id_riwayat_jabatan, status_supervisi){
    var id_ver=0;
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan_duplikasi&id_riwayat_jabatan=" + id_riwayat_jabatan;
}
    
function hapus(id_riwayat_jabatan, status_supervisi){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin ingin menghapus data riwayat jabatan ini?", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(r){
			var id_ver = 0;
			var modul_group = 4;
			var otorisasi = 0;
			if(id_ver==99 || modul_group==2 || status_supervisi<3 || (modul_group==1 && otorisasi==0)){
				jAlert("Data Berhasil Dihapus", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
					document.location.href="php/riwayat_jabatan/riwayat_jabatan_hapus.php?id_riwayat_jabatan=" + id_riwayat_jabatan;
				});	
			}else if(modul_group==1 && otorisasi==1){
				show_password_oto(id_riwayat_jabatan);
			}else{
				jAlert("Data Akan dilakukan Verifikasi Terlebih Dahulu Oleh Admin", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
					document.location.href="php/riwayat_jabatan/riwayat_jabatan_hapus.php?id_riwayat_jabatan=" + id_riwayat_jabatan;
				});	
			}
		}
	});	
}

function hapus_riwayat_tolak(){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin ingin menghapus seluruh riwayat tolak jabatan dari BKD? ", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			document.location.href="php/riwayat_jabatan/hapus_riwayat_tolak.php";
	});
}

function catatan(id_riwayat_jabatan){
    $("#dialog_cadis").dialog("open");
    $.ajax({
        type: "GET",
        url: "ajax/cadis_spv_riwayat_jabatan.php",
        data: "id_riwayat_jabatan=" + id_riwayat_jabatan,
        success: function(r){
            $("#dialog_cadis").html(r);
        }
    });
}

function tambah_jabatan(){
    document.location.href='?mod=riwayat_jabatan_tambah'; 
}

function something_wrong(what_is_wrong){
    jAlert(what_is_wrong, "PERHATIAN");
}

function integrasi(id_riwayat_jabatan){
	$("#id_riw_x").val(id_riwayat_jabatan);
	$("#integrasi").dialog("open");
}

function update_siasn(id_action){
	$("#id_action").val(id_action);
	if(id_action==1 || id_action==0){
		$("#frm_int").submit();
	}
}
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">

$(document).ready(function(){
   ambil_tanggal("tanggal_lahir");
   
   $("#frm").submit(function(){
        if(frm.nip.value == "" || frm.nama_pegawai.value == "" || frm.id_jenis_kepegawaian.value == 0 || frm.id_kedudukan_kepegawaian.value == 0 || frm.id_status_kepegawaian.value == 0 || frm.id_agama.value == 0 || frm.id_jenis_kelamin.value == 0){
			var pesan ="Maaf!! Data dibawah ini harus diisi : <ol>";
			if(frm.nip.value == "" ){
				pesan += "<li>NIP</li>";
				document.getElementById("nip").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			} 
			if( frm.nama_pegawai.value == "" ){
				pesan += "<li>Nama Pegawai</li>";
				document.getElementById("nama_pegawai").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			} 
			if( frm.id_jenis_kepegawaian.value == 0 ){
				pesan += "<li>Jenis Kepegawaian</li>" ;
				document.getElementById("id_jenis_kepegawaian").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			} 
			if( frm.id_kedudukan_kepegawaian.value == 0 ){
				pesan += "<li>Kedudukan Kepegawaian</li>" ;
				document.getElementById("id_kedudukan_kepegawaian").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			} 
			if( frm.id_status_kepegawaian.value == 0 ){
				pesan += "<li>Status Kepegawaian</li>" ;
				document.getElementById("id_status_kepegawaian").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			} 
			if( frm.id_agama.value == 0 ){
				pesan +=  "<li>Agama</li>";
				document.getElementById("id_agama").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			} 
			if( frm.id_jenis_kelamin.value == 0){
				pesan += "<li>Jenis Kelamin</li>" ;
				document.getElementById("id_jenis_kelamin").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			}
			pesan += "<ol>";
            jAlert(pesan, "PERHATIAN");
            return false;
        }
        
   }); 
   
   	$("#input_password").dialog({

    });

	$("#pnl_catatan").dialog("open");
}

function something_wrong(what_is_wrong) {
        jAlert(what_is_wrong, "PERHATIAN");
}

function success(ada){
    jAlert("Data pegawai berhasil disimpan", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai";
	}); 
}

function success_upload(){
    jAlert("File berhasil diupload", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai";
	}); 
}

function show_password_oto(){
	$("#id_pegawaix").val(id_pegawai);
	$("#input_password").dialog("open");
}

function hold(){
    jAlert("Data sementara berhasil disimpan untuk diotorisasi", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai";
	}); 
}   

function approval(id_app){
	$("#id_approval").val(id_app);
	if(id_app==1){
	jAlert("<b>Data akan diotorisasi dahulu oleh pejabat berwenang</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			$("#frm_edit").submit();
		});
	}else if(id_app==0){
		$("#frm_edit").submit();
	}
}

 function success_catatan(){
	jAlert("<b>Catatan berhasil disimpan.</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai&id_ver=99";
		});	
} 

function lanjut(){
		upload_pasfoto();
}

/*Fungsi Untuk Hapus*/
 function notif(){
    jAlert("<b>Data Hapus Ini Akan diVerifikasi Terlebih Dahulu Oleh Admin</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai";
    });
}
 function success_hapus(){
    jAlert("<b>Data Berhasil Dihapus</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai";
    });
}

 function hapus_pasfoto(){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin akan menghapus file upload ini?", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(r){
			var modul_user = 16;
			if(modul_user==1 || modul_user==100)
				jAlert("<b>Data Hapus Ini Akan diVerifikasi Terlebih Dahulu Oleh Admin</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
					document.location.href = "php/upload_data/hapus_pasfoto2.php?id_pegawai=" + id_pegawai;
				});
			else
				jAlert("<b>Data Berhasil Dihapus</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
					document.location.href = "php/upload_data/hapus_pasfoto2.php?id_pegawai=" + id_pegawai;
				});
		}
    });
 }

function upload(id_pendidikan){
	$("#id_pendidikan").val(id_pendidikan);
    $("#pnl_upload").dialog("open");
}

function catatan_upload(id_pendidikan,catatan_pend){
	$("#id_pendidikan_catatan").val(id_pendidikan);
	$("#catatan_upload").html(catatan_pend);
    $("#pnl_catatan").dialog("open");
} 

function show_password_oto(id_riwayat){
	var id_pegawai = "25368";
	$("#id_pegawaix").val(id_pegawai);
	$("#id_riwayatx").val(id_riwayat);
	$("#input_password").dialog("open");
}

function hold(){
    jAlert("Data sementara berhasil disimpan untuk diotorisasi", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan";
	}); 
}

function success(id){ //id dari otorisasi=3
    jAlert("Data berhasil dihapus", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan";
	}); 
}

function approval(id_app){
	$("#id_approval").val(id_app);
	if(id_app==1){
	jAlert("<b>Data akan diotorisasi dahulu oleh pejabat berwenang</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			$("#frm_edit").submit();
		});
	}else if(id_app==0){
		$("#frm_edit").submit();
	}
}

function success_upload(){
    jAlert("<b>File scan SK telah diupload</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan";
    });
} 

function update_upload(){
    jAlert("<b>File Anda Telah Diupdate</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan";
    });
}

function success_catatan(){
	var id_ver = 0;
	 jAlert("<b>Catatan tolak anda berhasil disimpan</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(id_ver==99)
			document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan&id_ver=99";
		else
			document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan";

		})
}

function edit(id_pendidikan){
	var id_ver="";
	if(id_ver==99){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan_edit&id_pendidikan=" + id_pendidikan + "&id_ver="+id_ver;
	}else{ 
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan_edit&id_pendidikan=" + id_pendidikan; 
	}
}
 
function hapus(id_pendidikan,status_spv){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin ingin menghapus data riwayat pendidikan ini?", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(r){
			var id_ver = 0;
			var modul_group = 4;
			var otorisasi = 0;
			if(id_ver==99 || modul_group==2 || status_spv<3 || (modul_group==1 && otorisasi==0)){
				jAlert("Data Berhasil Dihapus", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
				document.location.href="php/riwayat_pendidikan/riwayat_pendidikan_hapus.php?id_pendidikan=" + id_pendidikan + "&id_ver=" + id_ver;}
				);
			}else if(modul_group==1 && otorisasi==1){
				show_password_oto(id_pendidikan);
			}else{		
				jAlert("Data Akan dilakukan Verifikasi Terlebih Dahulu Oleh Admin", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
				document.location.href="php/riwayat_pendidikan/riwayat_pendidikan_hapus.php?id_pendidikan=" + id_pendidikan;
				});	
			}				
		}
	}); 
} 
 
function hapus_riwayat_tolak(){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin ingin menghapus seluruh riwayat tolak pendidikan dari BKD? ", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			document.location.href="php/riwayat_pendidikan/hapus_riwayat_tolak.php";
	});
}

function tambah_pendidikan(){
   document.location.href='?mod=riwayat_pendidikan_tambah';
}

function something_wrong(what_is_wrong){
    jAlert(what_is_wrong, "PERHATIAN");
}

		autoOpen: false,
		height: "auto",
		width: "800",
		modal: true,
		show: "fade",
		hide: "fade"
	});
	
	$( "#pnl_catatan" ).dialog({
		autoOpen: false,
		height: "auto",
		width: "800",
		modal: true,
		show: "fade",
		hide: "fade"
	});
});

function hold(){
    jAlert("Data sementara berhasil disimpan untuk diotorisasi", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pangkat";
	}); 
}   

function show_password_oto(id){
	$("#id_riwayatx").val(id);
	$("#id_pegawaix").val(id_pegawai);
	$("#input_password").dialog("open");
}   

function approval(id_app){
	$("#id_approval").val(id_app);
	if(id_app==1){
	jAlert("<b>Data akan diotorisasi dahulu oleh pejabat berwenang</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			$("#frm_edit").submit();
		});
	}else if(id_app==0){
		$("#frm_edit").submit();
	}
}


function upload(id_riwayat_pangkat){
	$("#id_riwayat_pangkat").val(id_riwayat_pangkat);
    $("#pnl_upload").dialog("open");
}

function catatan_upload(id_riwayat_pangkat_catatan,catatan_upload_pangkat){
		$("#id_riwayat_pangkat_catatan").val(id_riwayat_pangkat_catatan);
		$("#catatan_upload").html(catatan_upload_pangkat);
		$("#pnl_catatan").dialog("open");
}

function success(id){
	text='disimpan';
	if(id==3){
		text='dihapus';
	}
    jAlert("Data riwayat pangkat berhasil "+text, "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pangkat";
    });
}

function success_upload(){
    jAlert("<b>File scan SK telah diupload</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pangkat";
    });
}

function update_upload(){
    jAlert("<b>File Anda Telah Diupdate</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pangkat";
    });
}

function success_catatan(){
	var id_ver = 0;
	jAlert("Catatan Disimpan!", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(id_ver==99)
			document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pangkat&id_ver=99";
		else
			document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pangkat";
    });
}

function edit(id_riwayat_pangkat, status_supervisi){
	var id_ver="";
	if(id_ver==99){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pangkat_edit&id_riwayat_pangkat=" + id_riwayat_pangkat + "&id_ver="+id_ver;
		
	}else{ 
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pangkat_edit&id_riwayat_pangkat=" + id_riwayat_pangkat; 
	}
}

function hapus(id_riwayat_pangkat, status_supervisi){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin ingin menghapus data riwayat pangkat ini?", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(r){
			var id_ver = 0;
			var modul_group = 4;
			var otorisasi = 0;
			if(id_ver==99 || modul_group==2 || status_supervisi<3 || (modul_group==1 && otorisasi==0)){
				jAlert("Data Berhasil Dihapus", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
					document.location.href="php/riwayat_pangkat/riwayat_pangkat_hapus.php?id_riwayat_pangkat=" + id_riwayat_pangkat;
				});	
			}else if(modul_group==1 && otorisasi==1){
				show_password_oto(id_riwayat_pangkat);
			}else{	
				jAlert("Data Akan dilakukan Verifikasi Terlebih Dahulu Oleh Admin", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
					document.location.href="php/riwayat_pangkat/riwayat_pangkat_hapus.php?id_riwayat_pangkat=" + id_riwayat_pangkat;
				});	
			}
		}
	});
}

function hapus_riwayat_tolak(){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin ingin menghapus seluruh riwayat tolak pangkat dari BKD? ", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			document.location.href="php/riwayat_pangkat/hapus_riwayat_tolak.php";
	});
}

function catatan(id_riwayat_pangkat){
    $("#dialog_cadis").dialog("open");
    $.ajax({
        type: "GET",
        url: "ajax/cadis_spv_riwayat_pangkat.php",
        data: "id_riwayat_pangkat=" + id_riwayat_pangkat,
        success: function(r){
            $("#dialog_cadis").html(r);
        }
    });
}

function tambah_pangkat(){
    document.location.href='?mod=riwayat_pangkat_tambah';
}

function something_wrong(what_is_wrong){
    jAlert(what_is_wrong, "PERHATIAN");
}
</script>

$(document).ready(function(){

   $("#frm").submit(function(){
        if(frm.nip.value == "" || frm.nama_pegawai.value == "" || frm.id_jenis_kepegawaian.value == 0 || frm.id_kedudukan_kepegawaian.value == 0 || frm.id_status_kepegawaian.value == 0 || frm.id_agama.value == 0 || frm.id_jenis_kelamin.value == 0){
			var pesan ="Maaf!! Data dibawah ini harus diisi : <ol>";
			if(frm.nip.value == "" ){
				pesan += "<li>NIP</li>";
				document.getElementById("nip").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			} 
			if( frm.nama_pegawai.value == "" ){
				pesan += "<li>Nama Pegawai</li>";
				document.getElementById("nama_pegawai").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			} 
			if( frm.id_jenis_kepegawaian.value == 0 ){
				pesan += "<li>Jenis Kepegawaian</li>" ;
				document.getElementById("id_jenis_kepegawaian").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			} 
			if( frm.id_kedudukan_kepegawaian.value == 0 ){
				pesan += "<li>Kedudukan Kepegawaian</li>" ;
				document.getElementById("id_kedudukan_kepegawaian").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			} 
			if( frm.id_status_kepegawaian.value == 0 ){
				pesan += "<li>Status Kepegawaian</li>" ;
				document.getElementById("id_status_kepegawaian").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			} 
			if( frm.id_agama.value == 0 ){
				pesan +=  "<li>Agama</li>";
				document.getElementById("id_agama").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			} 
			if( frm.id_jenis_kelamin.value == 0){
				pesan += "<li>Jenis Kelamin</li>" ;
				document.getElementById("id_jenis_kelamin").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			}
			pesan += "<ol>";
            jAlert(pesan, "PERHATIAN");
            return false;
        }
        
   });

		$("#pnl_upload_1").dialog("open");
}

	}); 
}

	});

	$("#input_password").dialog("open");

    jAlert("Data sementara berhasil disimpan untuk diotorisasi", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai";
	}); 
}   

function approval(id_app){
	$("#id_approval").val(id_app);
	if(id_app==1){
	jAlert("<b>Data akan diotorisasi dahulu oleh pejabat berwenang</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			$("#frm_edit").submit();
		});
	}else if(id_app==0){
		$("#frm_edit").submit();
	}
}

	jAlert("<b>Catatan berhasil disimpan.</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai&id_ver=99";
		});	
}

        document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai";
    });
}

 function hapus_pasfoto(){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin akan menghapus file upload ini?", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(r){
			var modul_user = 16;
			if(modul_user==1 || modul_user==100)
				jAlert("<b>Data Hapus Ini Akan diVerifikasi Terlebih Dahulu Oleh Admin</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
					document.location.href = "php/upload_data/hapus_pasfoto2.php?id_pegawai=" + id_pegawai;
				});
			else
				jAlert("<b>Data Berhasil Dihapus</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
					document.location.href = "php/upload_data/hapus_pasfoto2.php?id_pegawai=" + id_pegawai;
				});
		}
    });
 }

$(document).ready(function(){
    $( "#pnl_upload" ).dialog({
            autoOpen: false,
    		height: "auto",
    		width: "800",
    		modal: true,
            show: "fade",
    		hide: "fade"
    });
	$( "#pnl_catatan" ).dialog({
            autoOpen: false,
    		height: "auto",
    		width: "800",
    		modal: true,
            show: "fade",
    		hide: "fade"
    });
	$("#input_password").dialog({
		autoOpen: false,
		height: "auto",
		width: "800",
		modal: true,
		show: "fade",
		hide: "fade"
	});	
});

function upload(id_pendidikan){
	$("#id_pendidikan").val(id_pendidikan);
    $("#pnl_upload").dialog("open");
}

function catatan_upload(id_pendidikan,catatan_pend){
	$("#id_pendidikan_catatan").val(id_pendidikan);
	$("#catatan_upload").html(catatan_pend);
    $("#pnl_catatan").dialog("open");
} 

function show_password_oto(id_riwayat){
	var id_pegawai = "22650";
	$("#id_pegawaix").val(id_pegawai);
	$("#id_riwayatx").val(id_riwayat);
	$("#input_password").dialog("open");
}

function hold(){
    jAlert("Data sementara berhasil disimpan untuk diotorisasi", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan";
	}); 
}

function success(id){ //id dari otorisasi=3
    jAlert("Data berhasil dihapus", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan";
	}); 
}

function approval(id_app){
	$("#id_approval").val(id_app);
	if(id_app==1){
	jAlert("<b>Data akan diotorisasi dahulu oleh pejabat berwenang</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			$("#frm_edit").submit();
		});
	}else if(id_app==0){
		$("#frm_edit").submit();
	}
}

function success_upload(){
    jAlert("<b>File scan SK telah diupload</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan";
    });
} 

function update_upload(){
    jAlert("<b>File Anda Telah Diupdate</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan";
    });
}

function success_catatan(){
	var id_ver = 0;
	 jAlert("<b>Catatan tolak anda berhasil disimpan</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(id_ver==99)
			document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan&id_ver=99";
		else
			document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan";

		})
}

function edit(id_pendidikan){
	var id_ver="";
	if(id_ver==99){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan_edit&id_pendidikan=" + id_pendidikan + "&id_ver="+id_ver;
	}else{ 
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan_edit&id_pendidikan=" + id_pendidikan; 
	}
}
 
function hapus(id_pendidikan,status_spv){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin ingin menghapus data riwayat pendidikan ini?", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(r){
			var id_ver = 0;
			var modul_group = 4;
			var otorisasi = 0;
			if(id_ver==99 || modul_group==2 || status_spv<3 || (modul_group==1 && otorisasi==0)){
				jAlert("Data Berhasil Dihapus", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
				document.location.href="php/riwayat_pendidikan/riwayat_pendidikan_hapus.php?id_pendidikan=" + id_pendidikan + "&id_ver=" + id_ver;}
				);
			}else if(modul_group==1 && otorisasi==1){
				show_password_oto(id_pendidikan);
			}else{		
				jAlert("Data Akan dilakukan Verifikasi Terlebih Dahulu Oleh Admin", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
				document.location.href="php/riwayat_pendidikan/riwayat_pendidikan_hapus.php?id_pendidikan=" + id_pendidikan;
				});	
			}				
		}
	}); 
} 
 
function hapus_riwayat_tolak(){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin ingin menghapus seluruh riwayat tolak pendidikan dari BKD? ", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			document.location.href="php/riwayat_pendidikan/hapus_riwayat_tolak.php";
	});
}

function tambah_pendidikan(){
   document.location.href='?mod=riwayat_pendidikan_tambah';
}

function something_wrong(what_is_wrong){
    jAlert(what_is_wrong, "PERHATIAN");
}
</script>

    jAlert("<b>File Anda Telah Diupdate</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pangkat";
    });
}

function success_catatan(){
	var id_ver = 0;
	jAlert("Catatan Disimpan!", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(id_ver==99)
			document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pangkat&id_ver=99";
		else
			document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pangkat";
    });
}

function edit(id_riwayat_pangkat, status_supervisi){
	var id_ver="";
	if(id_ver==99){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pangkat_edit&id_riwayat_pangkat=" + id_riwayat_pangkat + "&id_ver="+id_ver;
		
	}else{ 
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pangkat_edit&id_riwayat_pangkat=" + id_riwayat_pangkat; 
	}
}

function hapus(id_riwayat_pangkat, status_supervisi){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin ingin menghapus data riwayat pangkat ini?", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(r){
			var id_ver = 0;
			var modul_group = 4;
			var otorisasi = 0;
			if(id_ver==99 || modul_group==2 || status_supervisi<3 || (modul_group==1 && otorisasi==0)){
				jAlert("Data Berhasil Dihapus", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
					document.location.href="php/riwayat_pangkat/riwayat_pangkat_hapus.php?id_riwayat_pangkat=" + id_riwayat_pangkat;
				});	
			}else if(modul_group==1 && otorisasi==1){
				show_password_oto(id_riwayat_pangkat);
			}else{	
				jAlert("Data Akan dilakukan Verifikasi Terlebih Dahulu Oleh Admin", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
					document.location.href="php/riwayat_pangkat/riwayat_pangkat_hapus.php?id_riwayat_pangkat=" + id_riwayat_pangkat;
				});	
			}
		}
	});
}

function hapus_riwayat_tolak(){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin ingin menghapus seluruh riwayat tolak pangkat dari BKD? ", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			document.location.href="php/riwayat_pangkat/hapus_riwayat_tolak.php";
	});
}

function catatan(id_riwayat_pangkat){
    $("#dialog_cadis").dialog("open");
    $.ajax({
        type: "GET",
        url: "ajax/cadis_spv_riwayat_pangkat.php",
        data: "id_riwayat_pangkat=" + id_riwayat_pangkat,
        success: function(r){
            $("#dialog_cadis").html(r);
        }
    });
}

function tambah_pangkat(){
    document.location.href='?mod=riwayat_pangkat_tambah';
}

function something_wrong(what_is_wrong){
    jAlert(what_is_wrong, "PERHATIAN");
}

    jAlert("Data sementara berhasil disimpan untuk diotorisasi", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan";
	}); 
} 

function success(id){
	if(id==3){
		text = "Data berhasil dihapus.";
	}else if(id==4){
		text = "Data berhasil diupdate ke SIASN.";
	}
    jAlert(text, "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan";
	}); 
}   

function show_password_oto(id){
	$("#id_riwayatx").val(id);
	$("#id_pegawaix").val(id_pegawai);
	$("#input_password").dialog("open");
}   

function approval(id_app){
	$("#id_approval").val(id_app);
	if(id_app==1){
	jAlert("<b>Data akan diotorisasi dahulu oleh pejabat berwenang</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			$("#frm_edit").submit();
		});
	}else if(id_app==0){
		$("#frm_edit").submit();
	}
}

	$("#id_riwayat_jabatan").val(id_riwayat_jabatan);
    $("#pnl_upload").dialog("open");
}

function hapus(id_riwayat_jabatan, status_supervisi){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin ingin menghapus data riwayat jabatan ini?", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(r){
			var id_ver = 0;
			var modul_group = 4;
			var otorisasi = 0;
			if(id_ver==99 || modul_group==2 || status_supervisi<3 || (modul_group==1 && otorisasi==0)){
				jAlert("Data Berhasil Dihapus", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
					document.location.href="php/riwayat_jabatan/riwayat_jabatan_hapus.php?id_riwayat_jabatan=" + id_riwayat_jabatan;
				});	
			}else if(modul_group==1 && otorisasi==1){
				show_password_oto(id_riwayat_jabatan);
			}else{
				jAlert("Data Akan dilakukan Verifikasi Terlebih Dahulu Oleh Admin", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
					document.location.href="php/riwayat_jabatan/riwayat_jabatan_hapus.php?id_riwayat_jabatan=" + id_riwayat_jabatan;
				});	
			}
		}
	});	
}

$(document).ready(function(){

   $("#frm").submit(function(){
        if(frm.nip.value == "" || frm.nama_pegawai.value == "" || frm.id_jenis_kepegawaian.value == 0 || frm.id_kedudukan_kepegawaian.value == 0 || frm.id_status_kepegawaian.value == 0 || frm.id_agama.value == 0 || frm.id_jenis_kelamin.value == 0){
			var pesan ="Maaf!! Data dibawah ini harus diisi : <ol>";
			if(frm.nip.value == "" ){
				pesan += "<li>NIP</li>";
				document.getElementById("nip").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			} 
			if( frm.nama_pegawai.value == "" ){
				pesan += "<li>Nama Pegawai</li>";
				document.getElementById("nama_pegawai").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			} 
			if( frm.id_jenis_kepegawaian.value == 0 ){
				pesan += "<li>Jenis Kepegawaian</li>" ;
				document.getElementById("id_jenis_kepegawaian").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			} 
			if( frm.id_kedudukan_kepegawaian.value == 0 ){
				pesan += "<li>Kedudukan Kepegawaian</li>" ;
				document.getElementById("id_kedudukan_kepegawaian").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			} 
			if( frm.id_status_kepegawaian.value == 0 ){
				pesan += "<li>Status Kepegawaian</li>" ;
				document.getElementById("id_status_kepegawaian").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			} 
			if( frm.id_agama.value == 0 ){
				pesan +=  "<li>Agama</li>";
				document.getElementById("id_agama").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			} 
			if( frm.id_jenis_kelamin.value == 0){
				pesan += "<li>Jenis Kelamin</li>" ;
				document.getElementById("id_jenis_kelamin").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			}
			pesan += "<ol>";
            jAlert(pesan, "PERHATIAN");
            return false;
        }
        
   });

function upload_pasfoto(){ 
		$("#id_pegawai_5").val(id_pegawai);
		$("#pnl_upload_1").dialog("open");
}

		$("#id_pegawai_6").val(id_pegawai); 
		$("#pnl_upload_2").dialog("open");
}

function catatan_upload(id_pegawai){
	$("#id_pegawai2").val(id_pegawai);
	$("#catatan_karpeg_dan_pasfoto").html(catatan_karpeg_dan_pasfoto);
	$("#pnl_catatan").dialog("open");
}

function something_wrong(what_is_wrong) {
        jAlert(what_is_wrong, "PERHATIAN");
}

function success(ada){
    jAlert("Data pegawai berhasil disimpan", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai";
	}); 
}

function success_upload(){
    jAlert("File berhasil diupload", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai";
	}); 
}

function show_password_oto(){
	$("#id_pegawaix").val(id_pegawai);
	$("#input_password").dialog("open");
}

function hold(){
    jAlert("Data sementara berhasil disimpan untuk diotorisasi", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai";
	}); 
}   

function approval(id_app){
	$("#id_approval").val(id_app);
	if(id_app==1){
	jAlert("<b>Data akan diotorisasi dahulu oleh pejabat berwenang</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			$("#frm_edit").submit();
		});
	}else if(id_app==0){
		$("#frm_edit").submit();
	}
}

 function success_catatan(){
	jAlert("<b>Catatan berhasil disimpan.</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai&id_ver=99";
		});	
} 

function lanjut(){
		upload_pasfoto();
}

/*Fungsi Untuk Hapus*/
 function notif(){
    jAlert("<b>Data Hapus Ini Akan diVerifikasi Terlebih Dahulu Oleh Admin</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai";
    });
}
 function success_hapus(){
    jAlert("<b>Data Berhasil Dihapus</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai";
    });
}

function upload(id_pendidikan){
	$("#id_pendidikan").val(id_pendidikan);
    $("#pnl_upload").dialog("open");
}

function catatan_upload(id_pendidikan,catatan_pend){
	$("#id_pendidikan_catatan").val(id_pendidikan);
	$("#catatan_upload").html(catatan_pend);
    $("#pnl_catatan").dialog("open");
} 

function show_password_oto(id_riwayat){
	var id_pegawai = "20001";
	$("#id_pegawaix").val(id_pegawai);
	$("#id_riwayatx").val(id_riwayat);
	$("#input_password").dialog("open");
}

function hold(){
    jAlert("Data sementara berhasil disimpan untuk diotorisasi", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan";
	}); 
}

function success(id){ //id dari otorisasi=3
    jAlert("Data berhasil dihapus", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan";
	}); 
}

function approval(id_app){
	$("#id_approval").val(id_app);
	if(id_app==1){
	jAlert("<b>Data akan diotorisasi dahulu oleh pejabat berwenang</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			$("#frm_edit").submit();
		});
	}else if(id_app==0){
		$("#frm_edit").submit();
	}
}

function success_upload(){
    jAlert("<b>File scan SK telah diupload</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan";
    });
} 

function update_upload(){
    jAlert("<b>File Anda Telah Diupdate</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan";
    });
}

function success_catatan(){
	var id_ver = 0;
	 jAlert("<b>Catatan tolak anda berhasil disimpan</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(id_ver==99)
			document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan&id_ver=99";
		else
			document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan";

		})
}

function edit(id_pendidikan){
	var id_ver="";
	if(id_ver==99){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan_edit&id_pendidikan=" + id_pendidikan + "&id_ver="+id_ver;
	}else{ 
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan_edit&id_pendidikan=" + id_pendidikan; 
	}
}
 
function hapus(id_pendidikan,status_spv){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin ingin menghapus data riwayat pendidikan ini?", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(r){
			var id_ver = 0;
			var modul_group = 4;
			var otorisasi = 0;
			if(id_ver==99 || modul_group==2 || status_spv<3 || (modul_group==1 && otorisasi==0)){
				jAlert("Data Berhasil Dihapus", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
				document.location.href="php/riwayat_pendidikan/riwayat_pendidikan_hapus.php?id_pendidikan=" + id_pendidikan + "&id_ver=" + id_ver;}
				);
			}else if(modul_group==1 && otorisasi==1){
				show_password_oto(id_pendidikan);
			}else{		
				jAlert("Data Akan dilakukan Verifikasi Terlebih Dahulu Oleh Admin", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
				document.location.href="php/riwayat_pendidikan/riwayat_pendidikan_hapus.php?id_pendidikan=" + id_pendidikan;
				});	
			}				
		}
	}); 
} 
 
function hapus_riwayat_tolak(){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin ingin menghapus seluruh riwayat tolak pendidikan dari BKD? ", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			document.location.href="php/riwayat_pendidikan/hapus_riwayat_tolak.php";
	});
}

function tambah_pendidikan(){
   document.location.href='?mod=riwayat_pendidikan_tambah';
}

function something_wrong(what_is_wrong){
    jAlert(what_is_wrong, "PERHATIAN");
}

function hapus(id_riwayat_pangkat, status_supervisi){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin ingin menghapus data riwayat pangkat ini?", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(r){
			var id_ver = 0;
			var modul_group = 4;
			var otorisasi = 0;
			if(id_ver==99 || modul_group==2 || status_supervisi<3 || (modul_group==1 && otorisasi==0)){
				jAlert("Data Berhasil Dihapus", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
					document.location.href="php/riwayat_pangkat/riwayat_pangkat_hapus.php?id_riwayat_pangkat=" + id_riwayat_pangkat;
				});	
			}else if(modul_group==1 && otorisasi==1){
				show_password_oto(id_riwayat_pangkat);
			}else{	
				jAlert("Data Akan dilakukan Verifikasi Terlebih Dahulu Oleh Admin", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
					document.location.href="php/riwayat_pangkat/riwayat_pangkat_hapus.php?id_riwayat_pangkat=" + id_riwayat_pangkat;

		}
	});

var id_pegawai ="20001";

		hide: "fade"
    });

            autoOpen: false,

    		modal: true,
            show: "fade",
    		hide: "fade"
    });
	$("#pnl_catatan").dialog({
            autoOpen: false,
    		height: "auto",

    		modal: true,
            show: "fade",
    		hide: "fade"
    });
	
	$( "#pnl_upload" ).dialog({
            autoOpen: false,

            show: "fade",
    		hide: "fade"
    });
	$("#integrasi").dialog({
            autoOpen: false,

    		modal: true,
            show: "fade",
    		hide: "fade"
    });
});

function hold(){
    jAlert("Data sementara berhasil disimpan untuk diotorisasi", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan";
	}); 
} 

function success(id){
	if(id==3){
		text = "Data berhasil dihapus.";
	}else if(id==4){
		text = "Data berhasil diupdate ke SIASN.";
	}
    jAlert(text, "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan";
	}); 
}   

function show_password_oto(id){
	$("#id_riwayatx").val(id);
	$("#id_pegawaix").val(id_pegawai);
	$("#input_password").dialog("open");
}   

function approval(id_app){
	$("#id_approval").val(id_app);
	if(id_app==1){
	jAlert("<b>Data akan diotorisasi dahulu oleh pejabat berwenang</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			$("#frm_edit").submit();
		});
	}else if(id_app==0){
		$("#frm_edit").submit();
	}
}

function upload(id_riwayat_jabatan){
	$("#id_riwayat_jabatan").val(id_riwayat_jabatan);
    $("#pnl_upload").dialog("open");
}

function catatan_upload(id_riwayat_jabatan_catatan, catatan_upload_jabatan){
		$("#id_riwayat_jabatan_catatan").val(id_riwayat_jabatan_catatan);
		$("#catatan_upload").html(catatan_upload_jabatan);
		$("#pnl_catatan").dialog("open");
}

function success_upload(){
    jAlert("<b>File scan SK telah diupload</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan";
    });
}

function update_upload(){
    jAlert("<b>File Anda Telah Diupdate</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan";
    });
}

    });

function success_catatan(){
	var id_ver = 0;
    jAlert("Catatan Disimpan!", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(id_ver==99)
			document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan&id_ver=99";
		else
			document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan";
    });
}

function success_integrasi(info){
	jAlert(info, "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan";
    });
}

function edit(id_riwayat_jabatan, status_supervisi){
    var id_ver=0;
	if(id_ver==99){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan_edit&id_riwayat_jabatan=" + id_riwayat_jabatan + "&id_ver="+id_ver;
	}else{
        document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan_edit&id_riwayat_jabatan=" + id_riwayat_jabatan;
	}
}

function duplikasi(id_riwayat_jabatan, status_supervisi){
    var id_ver=0;
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan_duplikasi&id_riwayat_jabatan=" + id_riwayat_jabatan;
}
    
function hapus(id_riwayat_jabatan, status_supervisi){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin ingin menghapus data riwayat jabatan ini?", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(r){
			var id_ver = 0;
			var modul_group = 4;
			var otorisasi = 0;
			if(id_ver==99 || modul_group==2 || status_supervisi<3 || (modul_group==1 && otorisasi==0)){
				jAlert("Data Berhasil Dihapus", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
					document.location.href="php/riwayat_jabatan/riwayat_jabatan_hapus.php?id_riwayat_jabatan=" + id_riwayat_jabatan;
				});	
			}else if(modul_group==1 && otorisasi==1){
				show_password_oto(id_riwayat_jabatan);
			}else{
				jAlert("Data Akan dilakukan Verifikasi Terlebih Dahulu Oleh Admin", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
					document.location.href="php/riwayat_jabatan/riwayat_jabatan_hapus.php?id_riwayat_jabatan=" + id_riwayat_jabatan;
				});	
			}
		}
	});	
}

function hapus_riwayat_tolak(){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin ingin menghapus seluruh riwayat tolak jabatan dari BKD? ", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			document.location.href="php/riwayat_jabatan/hapus_riwayat_tolak.php";
	});
}

function catatan(id_riwayat_jabatan){
    $("#dialog_cadis").dialog("open");
    $.ajax({
        type: "GET",
        url: "ajax/cadis_spv_riwayat_jabatan.php",
        data: "id_riwayat_jabatan=" + id_riwayat_jabatan,
        success: function(r){
            $("#dialog_cadis").html(r);
        }
    });
}

function tambah_jabatan(){
    document.location.href='?mod=riwayat_jabatan_tambah'; 
}

function something_wrong(what_is_wrong){
    jAlert(what_is_wrong, "PERHATIAN");
}

function integrasi(id_riwayat_jabatan){
	$("#id_riw_x").val(id_riwayat_jabatan);
	$("#integrasi").dialog("open");
}

function update_siasn(id_action){
	$("#id_action").val(id_action);
	if(id_action==1 || id_action==0){
		$("#frm_int").submit();
	}
}

$(document).ready(function(){

   $("#frm").submit(function(){
        if(frm.nip.value == "" || frm.nama_pegawai.value == "" || frm.id_jenis_kepegawaian.value == 0 || frm.id_kedudukan_kepegawaian.value == 0 || frm.id_status_kepegawaian.value == 0 || frm.id_agama.value == 0 || frm.id_jenis_kelamin.value == 0){
			var pesan ="Maaf!! Data dibawah ini harus diisi : <ol>";
			if(frm.nip.value == "" ){
				pesan += "<li>NIP</li>";
				document.getElementById("nip").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			} 
			if( frm.nama_pegawai.value == "" ){
				pesan += "<li>Nama Pegawai</li>";
				document.getElementById("nama_pegawai").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			} 
			if( frm.id_jenis_kepegawaian.value == 0 ){
				pesan += "<li>Jenis Kepegawaian</li>" ;
				document.getElementById("id_jenis_kepegawaian").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			} 
			if( frm.id_kedudukan_kepegawaian.value == 0 ){
				pesan += "<li>Kedudukan Kepegawaian</li>" ;
				document.getElementById("id_kedudukan_kepegawaian").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			} 
			if( frm.id_status_kepegawaian.value == 0 ){
				pesan += "<li>Status Kepegawaian</li>" ;
				document.getElementById("id_status_kepegawaian").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			} 
			if( frm.id_agama.value == 0 ){
				pesan +=  "<li>Agama</li>";
				document.getElementById("id_agama").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			} 
			if( frm.id_jenis_kelamin.value == 0){
				pesan += "<li>Jenis Kelamin</li>" ;
				document.getElementById("id_jenis_kelamin").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			}
			pesan += "<ol>";
            jAlert(pesan, "PERHATIAN");
            return false;
        }
        
   });

		$("#pnl_upload_1").dialog("open");
}

function catatan_upload(id_pegawai){
	$("#id_pegawai2").val(id_pegawai);
	$("#catatan_karpeg_dan_pasfoto").html(catatan_karpeg_dan_pasfoto);
	$("#pnl_catatan").dialog("open");
}

function something_wrong(what_is_wrong) {
        jAlert(what_is_wrong, "PERHATIAN");
}

function success(ada){
    jAlert("Data pegawai berhasil disimpan", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai";
	}); 
}

function success_upload(){
    jAlert("File berhasil diupload", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai";
	}); 
}

function show_password_oto(){
	$("#id_pegawaix").val(id_pegawai);
	$("#input_password").dialog("open");
}

function hold(){
    jAlert("Data sementara berhasil disimpan untuk diotorisasi", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai";
	}); 
}   

function approval(id_app){
	$("#id_approval").val(id_app);
	if(id_app==1){
	jAlert("<b>Data akan diotorisasi dahulu oleh pejabat berwenang</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			$("#frm_edit").submit();
		});
	}else if(id_app==0){
		$("#frm_edit").submit();
	}
}

 function success_catatan(){
	jAlert("<b>Catatan berhasil disimpan.</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai&id_ver=99";
		});	
} 

function lanjut(){
		upload_pasfoto();
}

/*Fungsi Untuk Hapus*/
 function notif(){
    jAlert("<b>Data Hapus Ini Akan diVerifikasi Terlebih Dahulu Oleh Admin</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai";
    });
}
 function success_hapus(){
    jAlert("<b>Data Berhasil Dihapus</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai";
    });
}

 function hapus_pasfoto(){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin akan menghapus file upload ini?", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(r){
			var modul_user = 16;
			if(modul_user==1 || modul_user==100)
				jAlert("<b>Data Hapus Ini Akan diVerifikasi Terlebih Dahulu Oleh Admin</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
					document.location.href = "php/upload_data/hapus_pasfoto2.php?id_pegawai=" + id_pegawai;
				});
			else
				jAlert("<b>Data Berhasil Dihapus</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
					document.location.href = "php/upload_data/hapus_pasfoto2.php?id_pegawai=" + id_pegawai;
				});
		}
    });
 }

					document.location.href = "php/upload_data/hapus_konversi_nip.php?id_pegawai=" + id_pegawai;
				});
		}
    });
 }
 function hapus_npwp(){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin akan menghapus file upload ini?", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
    if(r){
		var modul_user = 16;
		if(modul_user==1 || modul_user==100)
			jAlert("<b>Data Hapus Ini Akan diVerifikasi Terlebih Dahulu Oleh Admin</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
				document.location.href = "php/upload_data/hapus_npwp.php?id_pegawai=" + id_pegawai;
			});
		else
			jAlert("<b>Data Berhasil Dihapus</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
				document.location.href = "php/upload_data/hapus_npwp.php?id_pegawai=" + id_pegawai;
			});
        }
    });
 }

function upload(id_pendidikan){
	$("#id_pendidikan").val(id_pendidikan);
    $("#pnl_upload").dialog("open");
}

function catatan_upload(id_pendidikan,catatan_pend){
	$("#id_pendidikan_catatan").val(id_pendidikan);
	$("#catatan_upload").html(catatan_pend);
    $("#pnl_catatan").dialog("open");
} 

function show_password_oto(id_riwayat){
	var id_pegawai = "23088";
	$("#id_pegawaix").val(id_pegawai);
	$("#id_riwayatx").val(id_riwayat);
	$("#input_password").dialog("open");
}

function hold(){
    jAlert("Data sementara berhasil disimpan untuk diotorisasi", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan";
	}); 
}

function success(id){ //id dari otorisasi=3
    jAlert("Data berhasil dihapus", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan";
	}); 
}

function approval(id_app){
	$("#id_approval").val(id_app);
	if(id_app==1){
	jAlert("<b>Data akan diotorisasi dahulu oleh pejabat berwenang</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			$("#frm_edit").submit();
		});
	}else if(id_app==0){
		$("#frm_edit").submit();
	}
}

function success_upload(){
    jAlert("<b>File scan SK telah diupload</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan";
    });
} 

function update_upload(){
    jAlert("<b>File Anda Telah Diupdate</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan";
    });

function success_catatan(){
	var id_ver = 0;
	 jAlert("<b>Catatan tolak anda berhasil disimpan</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(id_ver==99)
			document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan&id_ver=99";
		else
			document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan";

		})
}

function edit(id_pendidikan){
	var id_ver="";
	if(id_ver==99){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan_edit&id_pendidikan=" + id_pendidikan + "&id_ver="+id_ver;
	}else{ 
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan_edit&id_pendidikan=" + id_pendidikan; 
	}
}
 
function hapus(id_pendidikan,status_spv){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin ingin menghapus data riwayat pendidikan ini?", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(r){
			var id_ver = 0;
			var modul_group = 4;
			var otorisasi = 0;
			if(id_ver==99 || modul_group==2 || status_spv<3 || (modul_group==1 && otorisasi==0)){
				jAlert("Data Berhasil Dihapus", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
				document.location.href="php/riwayat_pendidikan/riwayat_pendidikan_hapus.php?id_pendidikan=" + id_pendidikan + "&id_ver=" + id_ver;}
				);
			}else if(modul_group==1 && otorisasi==1){
				show_password_oto(id_pendidikan);
			}else{		
				jAlert("Data Akan dilakukan Verifikasi Terlebih Dahulu Oleh Admin", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
				document.location.href="php/riwayat_pendidikan/riwayat_pendidikan_hapus.php?id_pendidikan=" + id_pendidikan;
				});	
			}				
		}
	}); 
} 
 
function hapus_riwayat_tolak(){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin ingin menghapus seluruh riwayat tolak pendidikan dari BKD? ", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			document.location.href="php/riwayat_pendidikan/hapus_riwayat_tolak.php";
	});
}

function tambah_pendidikan(){
   document.location.href='?mod=riwayat_pendidikan_tambah';
}

function something_wrong(what_is_wrong){
    jAlert(what_is_wrong, "PERHATIAN");
}

var id_pegawai ="23088";

$(document).ready(function(){
    $("#dialog_cadis").dialog({
        autoOpen: false,
		height: 540,
		width: 500,
		modal: true,
        show: "fade",
		hide: "fade"
    });
	$("#input_password").dialog({
            autoOpen: false,
    		height: "auto",
    		width: "800",
    		modal: true,
            show: "fade",
    		hide: "fade"
    });
	$("#pnl_catatan").dialog({
            autoOpen: false,
    		height: "auto",
    		width: "800",
    		modal: true,
            show: "fade",
    		hide: "fade"
    });
	
	$( "#pnl_upload" ).dialog({
            autoOpen: false,
    		height: "auto",
    		width: "800",
    		modal: true,
            show: "fade",
    		hide: "fade"
    });
	$("#integrasi").dialog({
            autoOpen: false,
    		height: "auto",
    		width: "500",
    		modal: true,
            show: "fade",
    		hide: "fade"
    });
});

function hold(){
    jAlert("Data sementara berhasil disimpan untuk diotorisasi", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan";
	}); 
} 

function success(id){
	if(id==3){
		text = "Data berhasil dihapus.";
	}else if(id==4){
		text = "Data berhasil diupdate ke SIASN.";
	}
    jAlert(text, "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan";
	}); 
}   

function show_password_oto(id){
	$("#id_riwayatx").val(id);
	$("#id_pegawaix").val(id_pegawai);
	$("#input_password").dialog("open");
}   

function approval(id_app){
	$("#id_approval").val(id_app);
	if(id_app==1){
	jAlert("<b>Data akan diotorisasi dahulu oleh pejabat berwenang</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			$("#frm_edit").submit();
		});
	}else if(id_app==0){
		$("#frm_edit").submit();
	}
}

function upload(id_riwayat_jabatan){
	$("#id_riwayat_jabatan").val(id_riwayat_jabatan);
    $("#pnl_upload").dialog("open");
}

function catatan_upload(id_riwayat_jabatan_catatan, catatan_upload_jabatan){
		$("#id_riwayat_jabatan_catatan").val(id_riwayat_jabatan_catatan);
		$("#catatan_upload").html(catatan_upload_jabatan);
		$("#pnl_catatan").dialog("open");
}

function success_upload(){
    jAlert("<b>File scan SK telah diupload</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan";
    });
}

function update_upload(){
    jAlert("<b>File Anda Telah Diupdate</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan";
    });
}

/* function file_sudah_ada(){
    jAlert("<b>File Upload Sudah Ada!</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan";
    });
} */

function success_catatan(){
	var id_ver = 0;
    jAlert("Catatan Disimpan!", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(id_ver==99)
			document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan&id_ver=99";
		else
			document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan";
    });
}

function success_integrasi(info){
	jAlert(info, "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan";
    });
}

function edit(id_riwayat_jabatan, status_supervisi){
    var id_ver=0;
	if(id_ver==99){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan_edit&id_riwayat_jabatan=" + id_riwayat_jabatan + "&id_ver="+id_ver;
	}else{
        document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan_edit&id_riwayat_jabatan=" + id_riwayat_jabatan;
	}
}

function duplikasi(id_riwayat_jabatan, status_supervisi){
    var id_ver=0;
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan_duplikasi&id_riwayat_jabatan=" + id_riwayat_jabatan;
}
    
function hapus(id_riwayat_jabatan, status_supervisi){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin ingin menghapus data riwayat jabatan ini?", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(r){
			var id_ver = 0;
			var modul_group = 4;
			var otorisasi = 0;
			if(id_ver==99 || modul_group==2 || status_supervisi<3 || (modul_group==1 && otorisasi==0)){
				jAlert("Data Berhasil Dihapus", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
					document.location.href="php/riwayat_jabatan/riwayat_jabatan_hapus.php?id_riwayat_jabatan=" + id_riwayat_jabatan;
				});	
			}else if(modul_group==1 && otorisasi==1){
				show_password_oto(id_riwayat_jabatan);
			}else{
				jAlert("Data Akan dilakukan Verifikasi Terlebih Dahulu Oleh Admin", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
					document.location.href="php/riwayat_jabatan/riwayat_jabatan_hapus.php?id_riwayat_jabatan=" + id_riwayat_jabatan;
				});	
			}
		}
	});	
}

function hapus_riwayat_tolak(){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin ingin menghapus seluruh riwayat tolak jabatan dari BKD? ", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			document.location.href="php/riwayat_jabatan/hapus_riwayat_tolak.php";
	});
}

function catatan(id_riwayat_jabatan){
    $("#dialog_cadis").dialog("open");
    $.ajax({
        type: "GET",
        url: "ajax/cadis_spv_riwayat_jabatan.php",
        data: "id_riwayat_jabatan=" + id_riwayat_jabatan,
        success: function(r){
            $("#dialog_cadis").html(r);
        }
    });
}

function tambah_jabatan(){
    document.location.href='?mod=riwayat_jabatan_tambah'; 
}

function something_wrong(what_is_wrong){
    jAlert(what_is_wrong, "PERHATIAN");
}

function integrasi(id_riwayat_jabatan){
	$("#id_riw_x").val(id_riwayat_jabatan);
	$("#integrasi").dialog("open");
}

function update_siasn(id_action){
	$("#id_action").val(id_action);
	if(id_action==1 || id_action==0){
		$("#frm_int").submit();
	}
}
</script>

$(document).ready(function(){

        if(frm.nip.value == "" || frm.nama_pegawai.value == "" || frm.id_jenis_kepegawaian.value == 0 || frm.id_kedudukan_kepegawaian.value == 0 || frm.id_status_kepegawaian.value == 0 || frm.id_agama.value == 0 || frm.id_jenis_kelamin.value == 0){
			var pesan ="Maaf!! Data dibawah ini harus diisi : <ol>";
			if(frm.nip.value == "" ){
				pesan += "<li>NIP</li>";
				document.getElementById("nip").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			} 
			if( frm.nama_pegawai.value == "" ){
				pesan += "<li>Nama Pegawai</li>";
				document.getElementById("nama_pegawai").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			} 
			if( frm.id_jenis_kepegawaian.value == 0 ){
				pesan += "<li>Jenis Kepegawaian</li>" ;
				document.getElementById("id_jenis_kepegawaian").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			} 
			if( frm.id_kedudukan_kepegawaian.value == 0 ){
				pesan += "<li>Kedudukan Kepegawaian</li>" ;
				document.getElementById("id_kedudukan_kepegawaian").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			} 
			if( frm.id_status_kepegawaian.value == 0 ){
				pesan += "<li>Status Kepegawaian</li>" ;
				document.getElementById("id_status_kepegawaian").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			} 
			if( frm.id_agama.value == 0 ){
				pesan +=  "<li>Agama</li>";
				document.getElementById("id_agama").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			} 
			if( frm.id_jenis_kelamin.value == 0){
				pesan += "<li>Jenis Kelamin</li>" ;
				document.getElementById("id_jenis_kelamin").style.borderColor = "red"; 
			}
			pesan += "<ol>";
            jAlert(pesan, "PERHATIAN");
            return false;
        }
        
   });

	$("#id_pegawai2").val(id_pegawai);
	$("#catatan_karpeg_dan_pasfoto").html(catatan_karpeg_dan_pasfoto);
	$("#pnl_catatan").dialog("open");
}

function something_wrong(what_is_wrong) {
        jAlert(what_is_wrong, "PERHATIAN");
}

function success(ada){
    jAlert("Data pegawai berhasil disimpan", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai";
	}); 
}

function success_upload(){
    jAlert("File berhasil diupload", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai";
	}); 
}

function show_password_oto(){
	$("#id_pegawaix").val(id_pegawai);
	$("#input_password").dialog("open");
}

function hold(){
    jAlert("Data sementara berhasil disimpan untuk diotorisasi", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai";
	}); 
}   

function approval(id_app){
	$("#id_approval").val(id_app);
	if(id_app==1){
	jAlert("<b>Data akan diotorisasi dahulu oleh pejabat berwenang</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			$("#frm_edit").submit();
		});
	}else if(id_app==0){
		$("#frm_edit").submit();
	}
}

 function success_catatan(){
	jAlert("<b>Catatan berhasil disimpan.</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai&id_ver=99";
		});	
} 

function lanjut(){
		upload_pasfoto();
}

/*Fungsi Untuk Hapus*/
 function notif(){
    jAlert("<b>Data Hapus Ini Akan diVerifikasi Terlebih Dahulu Oleh Admin</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai";
    });
}
 function success_hapus(){
    jAlert("<b>Data Berhasil Dihapus</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=edit_pegawai";
    });
}

 function hapus_pasfoto(){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin akan menghapus file upload ini?", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(r){
			var modul_user = 16;
			if(modul_user==1 || modul_user==100)
				jAlert("<b>Data Hapus Ini Akan diVerifikasi Terlebih Dahulu Oleh Admin</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
					document.location.href = "php/upload_data/hapus_pasfoto2.php?id_pegawai=" + id_pegawai;
				});
			else
				jAlert("<b>Data Berhasil Dihapus</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
					document.location.href = "php/upload_data/hapus_pasfoto2.php?id_pegawai=" + id_pegawai;
				});
		}
    });
 }

			jAlert("<b>Data Berhasil Dihapus</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
				document.location.href = "php/upload_data/hapus_taspen.php?id_pegawai=" + id_pegawai;
			});
        }
    });
 }
 function hapus_kelahiran(){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin akan menghapus file upload ini?", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
    if(r){
		var modul_user = 16;
		if(modul_user==1 || modul_user==100)
			jAlert("<b>Data Hapus Ini Akan diVerifikasi Terlebih Dahulu Oleh Admin</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
				document.location.href = "php/upload_data/hapus_akta_kelahiran.php?id_pegawai=" + id_pegawai;
			});
		else
			jAlert("<b>Data Berhasil Dihapus</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
				document.location.href = "php/upload_data/hapus_akta_kelahiran.php?id_pegawai=" + id_pegawai;
			});
        }
    });
 }

		var id_author=0;
		if(id_author>0){
			document.location.href="?mod=otorisasi_data";
		}else{

		}

function upload(id_pendidikan){
	$("#id_pendidikan").val(id_pendidikan);
    $("#pnl_upload").dialog("open");
}

function catatan_upload(id_pendidikan,catatan_pend){
	$("#id_pendidikan_catatan").val(id_pendidikan);
	$("#catatan_upload").html(catatan_pend);
    $("#pnl_catatan").dialog("open");
} 

function show_password_oto(id_riwayat){
	var id_pegawai = "25948";
	$("#id_pegawaix").val(id_pegawai);
	$("#id_riwayatx").val(id_riwayat);
	$("#input_password").dialog("open");
}

function hold(){
    jAlert("Data sementara berhasil disimpan untuk diotorisasi", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan";
	}); 
}

function success(id){ //id dari otorisasi=3
    jAlert("Data berhasil dihapus", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan";
	}); 
}

function approval(id_app){
	$("#id_approval").val(id_app);
	if(id_app==1){
	jAlert("<b>Data akan diotorisasi dahulu oleh pejabat berwenang</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			$("#frm_edit").submit();
		});
	}else if(id_app==0){
		$("#frm_edit").submit();
	}
}

function success_upload(){
    jAlert("<b>File scan SK telah diupload</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan";
    });
} 

function update_upload(){
    jAlert("<b>File Anda Telah Diupdate</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan";
    });
}

function success_catatan(){
	var id_ver = 0;
	 jAlert("<b>Catatan tolak anda berhasil disimpan</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(id_ver==99)
			document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan&id_ver=99";
		else
			document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan";

		})
}

function edit(id_pendidikan){
	var id_ver="";
	if(id_ver==99){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan_edit&id_pendidikan=" + id_pendidikan + "&id_ver="+id_ver;
	}else{ 
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pendidikan_edit&id_pendidikan=" + id_pendidikan; 
	}
}
 
function hapus(id_pendidikan,status_spv){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin ingin menghapus data riwayat pendidikan ini?", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(r){
			var id_ver = 0;
			var modul_group = 4;
			var otorisasi = 0;
			if(id_ver==99 || modul_group==2 || status_spv<3 || (modul_group==1 && otorisasi==0)){
				jAlert("Data Berhasil Dihapus", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
				document.location.href="php/riwayat_pendidikan/riwayat_pendidikan_hapus.php?id_pendidikan=" + id_pendidikan + "&id_ver=" + id_ver;}
				);
			}else if(modul_group==1 && otorisasi==1){
				show_password_oto(id_pendidikan);
			}else{		
				jAlert("Data Akan dilakukan Verifikasi Terlebih Dahulu Oleh Admin", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
				document.location.href="php/riwayat_pendidikan/riwayat_pendidikan_hapus.php?id_pendidikan=" + id_pendidikan;
				});	
			}				
		}
	}); 
} 
 
function hapus_riwayat_tolak(){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin ingin menghapus seluruh riwayat tolak pendidikan dari BKD? ", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			document.location.href="php/riwayat_pendidikan/hapus_riwayat_tolak.php";
	});
}

function tambah_pendidikan(){
   document.location.href='?mod=riwayat_pendidikan_tambah';
}

function something_wrong(what_is_wrong){
    jAlert(what_is_wrong, "PERHATIAN");
}
</script>

var id_pegawai = "25948";
