function hold(){
    jAlert("Data sementara berhasil disimpan untuk diotorisasi", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pangkat";
	}); 
}   

function show_password_oto(id){
	$("#id_riwayatx").val(id);
	$("#id_pegawaix").val(id_pegawai);
	$("#input_password").dialog("open");
}   

function approval(id_app){
	$("#id_approval").val(id_app);
	if(id_app==1){
	jAlert("<b>Data akan diotorisasi dahulu oleh pejabat berwenang</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			$("#frm_edit").submit();
		});
	}else if(id_app==0){
		$("#frm_edit").submit();
	}
}


function upload(id_riwayat_pangkat){
	$("#id_riwayat_pangkat").val(id_riwayat_pangkat);
    $("#pnl_upload").dialog("open");
}

function catatan_upload(id_riwayat_pangkat_catatan,catatan_upload_pangkat){
		$("#id_riwayat_pangkat_catatan").val(id_riwayat_pangkat_catatan);
		$("#catatan_upload").html(catatan_upload_pangkat);
		$("#pnl_catatan").dialog("open");
}

function success(id){
	text='disimpan';
	if(id==3){
		text='dihapus';
	}
    jAlert("Data riwayat pangkat berhasil "+text, "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pangkat";
    });
}

function success_upload(){
    jAlert("<b>File scan SK telah diupload</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pangkat";
    });
}

function update_upload(){
    jAlert("<b>File Anda Telah Diupdate</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pangkat";
    });
}

function success_catatan(){
	var id_ver = 0;
	jAlert("Catatan Disimpan!", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(id_ver==99)
			document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pangkat&id_ver=99";
		else
			document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pangkat";
    });
}

function edit(id_riwayat_pangkat, status_supervisi){
	var id_ver="";
	if(id_ver==99){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pangkat_edit&id_riwayat_pangkat=" + id_riwayat_pangkat + "&id_ver="+id_ver;
		
	}else{ 
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_pangkat_edit&id_riwayat_pangkat=" + id_riwayat_pangkat; 
	}
}

function hapus(id_riwayat_pangkat, status_supervisi){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin ingin menghapus data riwayat pangkat ini?", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(r){
			var id_ver = 0;
			var modul_group = 4;
			var otorisasi = 0;
			if(id_ver==99 || modul_group==2 || status_supervisi<3 || (modul_group==1 && otorisasi==0)){
				jAlert("Data Berhasil Dihapus", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
					document.location.href="php/riwayat_pangkat/riwayat_pangkat_hapus.php?id_riwayat_pangkat=" + id_riwayat_pangkat;
				});	
			}else if(modul_group==1 && otorisasi==1){
				show_password_oto(id_riwayat_pangkat);
			}else{	
				jAlert("Data Akan dilakukan Verifikasi Terlebih Dahulu Oleh Admin", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
					document.location.href="php/riwayat_pangkat/riwayat_pangkat_hapus.php?id_riwayat_pangkat=" + id_riwayat_pangkat;
				});	
			}
		}
	});
}

function hapus_riwayat_tolak(){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin ingin menghapus seluruh riwayat tolak pangkat dari BKD? ", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			document.location.href="php/riwayat_pangkat/hapus_riwayat_tolak.php";
	});
}

function catatan(id_riwayat_pangkat){
    $("#dialog_cadis").dialog("open");
    $.ajax({
        type: "GET",
        url: "ajax/cadis_spv_riwayat_pangkat.php",
        data: "id_riwayat_pangkat=" + id_riwayat_pangkat,
        success: function(r){
            $("#dialog_cadis").html(r);
        }
    });
}

function tambah_pangkat(){
    document.location.href='?mod=riwayat_pangkat_tambah';
}

function something_wrong(what_is_wrong){
    jAlert(what_is_wrong, "PERHATIAN");
}
</script>

function hold(){
    jAlert("Data sementara berhasil disimpan untuk diotorisasi", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan";
	}); 
} 

function success(id){
	if(id==3){
		text = "Data berhasil dihapus.";
	}else if(id==4){
		text = "Data berhasil diupdate ke SIASN.";
	}
    jAlert(text, "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan";
	}); 
}   

function show_password_oto(id){
	$("#id_riwayatx").val(id);
	$("#id_pegawaix").val(id_pegawai);
	$("#input_password").dialog("open");
}   

function approval(id_app){
	$("#id_approval").val(id_app);
	if(id_app==1){
	jAlert("<b>Data akan diotorisasi dahulu oleh pejabat berwenang</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			$("#frm_edit").submit();
		});
	}else if(id_app==0){
		$("#frm_edit").submit();
	}
}

function upload(id_riwayat_jabatan){
	$("#id_riwayat_jabatan").val(id_riwayat_jabatan);
    $("#pnl_upload").dialog("open");
}

function catatan_upload(id_riwayat_jabatan_catatan, catatan_upload_jabatan){
		$("#id_riwayat_jabatan_catatan").val(id_riwayat_jabatan_catatan);
		$("#catatan_upload").html(catatan_upload_jabatan);
		$("#pnl_catatan").dialog("open");
}

function success_upload(){
    jAlert("<b>File scan SK telah diupload</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan";
    });
}

function update_upload(){
    jAlert("<b>File Anda Telah Diupdate</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan";
    });
}

    jAlert("<b>File Upload Sudah Ada!</b>", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
        document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan";
    });
} */

function success_catatan(){
	var id_ver = 0;
    jAlert("Catatan Disimpan!", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(id_ver==99)
			document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan&id_ver=99";
		else
			document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan";
    });
}

function success_integrasi(info){
	jAlert(info, "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan";
    });
}

function edit(id_riwayat_jabatan, status_supervisi){
    var id_ver=0;
	if(id_ver==99){
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan_edit&id_riwayat_jabatan=" + id_riwayat_jabatan + "&id_ver="+id_ver;
	}else{
        document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan_edit&id_riwayat_jabatan=" + id_riwayat_jabatan;
	}
}

function duplikasi(id_riwayat_jabatan, status_supervisi){
    var id_ver=0;
		document.location.href="?mod=riwayat_jabatan_duplikasi&id_riwayat_jabatan=" + id_riwayat_jabatan;
}
    
function hapus(id_riwayat_jabatan, status_supervisi){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin ingin menghapus data riwayat jabatan ini?", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
		if(r){
			var id_ver = 0;
			var modul_group = 4;
			var otorisasi = 0;
			if(id_ver==99 || modul_group==2 || status_supervisi<3 || (modul_group==1 && otorisasi==0)){
				jAlert("Data Berhasil Dihapus", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
					document.location.href="php/riwayat_jabatan/riwayat_jabatan_hapus.php?id_riwayat_jabatan=" + id_riwayat_jabatan;
				});	
			}else if(modul_group==1 && otorisasi==1){
				show_password_oto(id_riwayat_jabatan);
			}else{
				jAlert("Data Akan dilakukan Verifikasi Terlebih Dahulu Oleh Admin", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
					document.location.href="php/riwayat_jabatan/riwayat_jabatan_hapus.php?id_riwayat_jabatan=" + id_riwayat_jabatan;
				});	
			}
		}
	});	
}

function hapus_riwayat_tolak(){
	jConfirm("Anda yakin ingin menghapus seluruh riwayat tolak jabatan dari BKD? ", "PERHATIAN", function(r){
			document.location.href="php/riwayat_jabatan/hapus_riwayat_tolak.php";
	});
}

function catatan(id_riwayat_jabatan){
    $("#dialog_cadis").dialog("open");
    $.ajax({
        type: "GET",
        url: "ajax/cadis_spv_riwayat_jabatan.php",
        data: "id_riwayat_jabatan=" + id_riwayat_jabatan,
        success: function(r){
            $("#dialog_cadis").html(r);
        }
    });
}

function tambah_jabatan(){
    document.location.href='?mod=riwayat_jabatan_tambah'; 
}

function something_wrong(what_is_wrong){
    jAlert(what_is_wrong, "PERHATIAN");
}

function integrasi(id_riwayat_jabatan){
	$("#id_riw_x").val(id_riwayat_jabatan);
	$("#integrasi").dialog("open");
}

function update_siasn(id_action){
	$("#id_action").val(id_action);
	if(id_action==1 || id_action==0){
		$("#frm_int").submit();
	}
}
